µg/L; usually consistent\n    }\n\n    return ranges\n\ndef harmonize_value(value, feature_name, ranges_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Harmonize a single value based on physiological ranges and unit conversions.\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    if feature_name not in ranges_dict:\n        return value\n\n    min_val, max_val, conversion_factor, threshold = ranges_dict[feature_name]\n\n    # Check if value is within normal range\n    if min_val <= value <= max_val:\n        return value\n\n    # Check if conversion is needed\n    if threshold is not None:\n        # Determine conversion direction based on threshold and conversion factor\n        if conversion_factor > 1:\n            # Dividing conversion (e.g., µmol/L to mg/dL for creatinine)\n            if value > threshold:\n                converted = value / conversion_factor\n                if min_val <= converted <= max_val:\n                    return converted\n        else:\n            # Multiplying conversion (e.g., mmol/L to mg/dL)\n            if value < threshold:\n                converted = value / conversion_factor\n                if min_val <= converted <= max_val:\n                    return converted\n\n    # For some tests, we need to check both directions\n    if conversion_factor != 1 and threshold is not None:\n        # Try multiplying\n        converted_mult = value * conversion_factor\n        if min_val <= converted_mult <= max_val:\n            return converted_mult\n\n        # Try dividing\n        converted_div = value / conversion_factor\n        if min_val <= converted_div <= max_val:\n            return converted_div\n\n    # If still out of range, return original value (will be handled later)\n    return value\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading CKD lab data...\")\n\n    # Step 3: Get physiological ranges and conversion factors\n    ranges_dict = get_physiological_ranges_and_conversions()\n\n    # Step 4: Harmonize units for each feature\n    print(f\"\\nHarmonizing units based on physiological ranges...\")\n\n    for col in feature_columns:\n        if col in ranges_dict:\n            df_clean[col] = df_clean[col].apply(\n                lambda x: harmonize_value(x, col, ranges_dict)\n            )\n\n    # Step 5: Round all numeric columns to 2 decimal places\n    print(f\"\\nRounding all values to 2 decimal places...\")\n    for col in feature_columns:\n        df_clean[col] = df_clean[col].round(2)\n\n    # Step 6: Format to ensure exactly 2 decimal places\n    for col in feature_columns:\n        df_clean[col] = df_clean[col].apply(lambda x: f\"{x:.2f}\" if pd.notna(x) else x)\n\n    # Step 7: Save to output file\n    output_path = '/root/ckd_lab_data_harmonized.csv'\n    print(f\"\\nSaving harmonized data to {output_path}...\")\n    df_clean.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n\n    print(f\"\\n✓ Harmonization complete!\")\n    print(f\"  Final data shape: {df_clean.shape}\")\n    print(f\"  Output file: {output_path}\")\n\n    #
